- **aws-compliance.benchmark.nist_csf / aws-compliance.control.rds_db_instance_and_cluster_enhanced_monitoring_enabled**: `MAPPED | native: aws.cb.rds.cluster_log_exports_declared, aws.cb.rds.instance_enhanced_monitoring_except_m1_small | new: none` → `PARTIAL | native: aws.cb.rds.instance_enhanced_monitoring_except_m1_small | new: none`. The rds_cluster evaluator tests EnabledCloudwatchLogsExports, which is log export rather than Enhanced Monitoring. The RDS instance evaluator covers only instances; cluster coverage is unresolved.
- **aws-compliance.benchmark.nist_csf / aws-compliance.control.cloudfront_distribution_configured_with_origin_failover**: `MAPPED | native: aws.b.cloudfront.origin_group_metadata | new: none` → `NEW_CHECK | native: none | new: aws_cloudfront_origin_failover_configured`. The existing evaluator only tests OriginGroups.Quantity and item metadata; it does not verify distinct primary and secondary origins or failover criteria.
- **aws-compliance.benchmark.nist_csf / aws-compliance.control.eks_cluster_with_latest_kubernetes_version**: `MAPPED | native: aws.c2b.eks.version_at_least_1_19 | new: none` → `NEW_CHECK | native: none | new: aws_eks_cluster_current_supported_version`. The mapped evaluator uses the pinned version floor 1.19, which does not test the current approved EKS version.
- **aws-compliance.benchmark.nist_csf_v2 / aws-compliance.control.rds_db_instance_and_cluster_enhanced_monitoring_enabled**: `MAPPED | native: aws.cb.rds.cluster_log_exports_declared, aws.cb.rds.instance_enhanced_monitoring_except_m1_small | new: none` → `PARTIAL | native: aws.cb.rds.instance_enhanced_monitoring_except_m1_small | new: none`. The rds_cluster evaluator tests EnabledCloudwatchLogsExports, which is log export rather than Enhanced Monitoring. The RDS instance evaluator covers only instances; cluster coverage is unresolved.
- **aws-compliance.benchmark.nist_csf_v2 / aws-compliance.control.redshift_cluster_automatic_upgrade_major_versions_enabled**: `MAPPED | native: aws.cb.redshift.allow_version_upgrade | new: none` → `MANUAL | native: none | new: none`. AllowVersionUpgrade only permits Redshift maintenance version updates; it does not prove automatic major-version upgrades or an upgrade schedule.
- **aws-compliance.benchmark.nist_csf_v2 / aws-compliance.control.guardduty_no_high_severity_findings**: `MAPPED | native: aws.claude_a.guardduty.detector_no_findings | new: none` → `NEW_CHECK | native: none | new: aws_guardduty_no_active_high_findings`. The detector_no_findings evaluator counts all findings, including low severity. Severity and archive state require a finding-level read-only predicate.
- **aws-compliance.benchmark.nydfs_23 / aws-compliance.control.rds_db_instance_and_cluster_enhanced_monitoring_enabled**: `MAPPED | native: aws.cb.rds.cluster_log_exports_declared, aws.cb.rds.instance_enhanced_monitoring_except_m1_small | new: none` → `PARTIAL | native: aws.cb.rds.instance_enhanced_monitoring_except_m1_small | new: none`. The rds_cluster evaluator tests EnabledCloudwatchLogsExports, which is log export rather than Enhanced Monitoring. The RDS instance evaluator covers only instances; cluster coverage is unresolved.
- **ffiec_aws / d5-dr-de-b-2**: `PARTIAL | native: aws.a.cloudwatch.alarm_actions_enabled | new: aws_cloudwatch_performance_dashboard` → `MANUAL | native: none | new: none`. CloudWatch alarm ActionsEnabled and the presence of a dashboard metric do not establish that a system performance report exists or is used as an incident risk indicator.
- **nist_csf_2.0_aws / ae_5**: `PARTIAL | native: aws.a.cloudwatch.alarm_actions_enabled | new: aws_cloudwatch_metric_alarm_has_threshold` → `PARTIAL | native: none | new: aws_cloudwatch_metric_alarm_has_threshold`. ActionsEnabled tests whether an alarm can invoke actions, not whether an incident threshold is established. The CloudWatch Threshold/ComparisonOperator signal remains partial.

## Spot-check notes

- `peaso/checks/claude_aws_b_db.py:276`: RDS cluster evaluator reads `EnabledCloudwatchLogsExports`, which is log export, not Enhanced Monitoring.
- `peaso/checks/claude_aws_a_security.py:143`: detector-wide `FindingIds` count does not filter severity or archive state.
- `peaso/checks/claude2_aws_b_compute.py:664`: EKS evaluator compares with static `(1, 19)`, not the current supported version.
- `peaso/checks/aws_b_cloudfront.py:78`: origin-group check only reads declared metadata; member origins and failover codes are unverified.
- `peaso/checks/aws_a_regional.py:33`: Config recorder enabled evaluates recording status, not all-supported-resource coverage.
- `peaso/checks/aws_a_cloudwatch.py:13`: alarm action flag does not read metric thresholds or performance reports.
- `peaso/collectors/aws_facts_cb_ca.py:616`: Organizations master account ID is collected; a configured expected ID is required for comparison.
- `peaso/collectors/aws_facts_compute_edge.py:198`: launch configuration UserData is stripped, so the proposed credential signal needs a new in-memory projection.
- `peaso/collectors/aws_b_services.py:223`: AWS Backup plan details and selections are collected; a CopyAction alone does not prove a completed backup.
- `peaso/collectors/aws.py:631`: Config recorder payload comes from DescribeConfigurationRecorders; coverage fields are available.
- Organizational approvals, training, executive accountability, and provider-operated physical controls remain MANUAL or NOT_APPLICABLE as assigned where AWS configuration cannot prove them.

## Public snapshot mapping fixes

- **aws-compliance.benchmark.fedramp_low_rev_4 / aws-compliance.control.rds_db_snapshot_prohibit_public_access**: `MAPPED | aws.cb.rds.cluster_snapshot_restore_not_all_exact, aws.cb.rds.instance_snapshot_restore_not_all_exact` → `MAPPING_FIX | aws.cb.rds.cluster_snapshot_not_public_prowler, aws.cb.rds.instance_snapshot_not_public_prowler`. ## New-check specification corrections

- **nist_800_53_revision_4_aws / au_9_2**: `aws_backup_plan_cross_region_copy` (existing backup_plan.Plan from backup:GetBackupPlan and backup_protected_resource/recovery_point from backup:ListProtectedResources and backup:ListRecoveryPointsByResource; cloudtrail.S3BucketName from cloudtrail:DescribeTrails; backup selection detail via backup:GetBackupSelection; PASS only when each in-scope CloudTrail destination bucket has a completed AWS Backup recovery point copied to a vault in another AWS region within the configured maximum age; FAIL when a resolved bucket has no qualifying copy; UNKNOWN when bucket scope, recovery points, copy completion, or frequency policy is unavailable.) → `aws_backup_plan_cross_region_copy` (existing backup_plan.Plan from backup:GetBackupPlan, backup selection from backup:GetBackupSelection, CloudTrail destination from cloudtrail:DescribeTrails; add read-only backup:ListBackupVaults and backup:ListRecoveryPointsByBackupVault in source and destination regions to observe completed copies; PASS only when each in-scope CloudTrail destination bucket has a completed recovery point in a different-region vault with matching bucket ResourceArn and CreationDate within the configured maximum age; FAIL for a resolved bucket lacking such a point; UNKNOWN when region, bucket, copy status, recovery point inventory, or approved maximum age is unavailable.). Corrected resource coverage, read-only API provenance, or the shared check ID to match the predicate.
- **nist_csf_1.1_aws / am_5**: `aws_resource_classification_tag_present` (existing collected EC2, S3, RDS, Lambda and other tagged resource types + Tags/TagList plus configured classification tag key; PASS when every in-scope collected taggable resource has both an approved nonempty classification value and an approved nonempty criticality or business-value value under configured tag keys; FAIL when any resource lacks either value; UNKNOWN when approved keys or resource/tag coverage are unavailable.) → `aws_resource_priority_tags_present` (existing collected EC2, S3, RDS, Lambda and other tagged resource types plus Tags/TagList; configured classification and criticality/business-value tag keys and approved values; PASS when every in-scope collected taggable resource has both an approved nonempty classification value and an approved nonempty criticality or business-value value under configured tag keys; FAIL when any resource lacks either value; UNKNOWN when approved keys or resource/tag coverage are unavailable.). Corrected resource coverage, read-only API provenance, or the shared check ID to match the predicate.
- **nist_csf_2.0_aws / am_5**: `aws_resource_classification_tag_present` (existing collected EC2, S3, RDS, Lambda and other tagged resource types + Tags/TagList plus configured classification tag key; PASS when every in-scope collected taggable resource has both an approved nonempty classification value and an approved nonempty criticality or business-value value under configured tag keys; FAIL when any resource lacks either value; UNKNOWN when approved keys or resource/tag coverage are unavailable.) → `aws_resource_priority_tags_present` (existing collected EC2, S3, RDS, Lambda and other tagged resource types plus Tags/TagList; configured classification and criticality/business-value tag keys and approved values; PASS when every in-scope collected taggable resource has both an approved nonempty classification value and an approved nonempty criticality or business-value value under configured tag keys; FAIL when any resource lacks either value; UNKNOWN when approved keys or resource/tag coverage are unavailable.). Corrected resource coverage, read-only API provenance, or the shared check ID to match the predicate.

`nice -n 15 python3 docs/compliance-ledgers/.work/aws-nist-ffiec-nydfs/validate.py` completed successfully: 2,041 unique requirement decisions, 5,385 native-check references resolving to 580 indexed AWS IDs, 11 consistent shared new-check definitions, and 2,041 matching checklist rows. These source-text contradictions remain noted as doubts, not silent mapping changes.
